  const [loadingDocs, setLoadingDocs] = useState(false);
  
  // Formulários de Campo
  const [meterValue, setMeterValue] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingMeter, setSubmittingMeter] = useState(false);
  const [meterSuccess, setMeterSuccess] = useState(false);
  
  const [reportIssue, setReportIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueTitle, setIssueTitle] = useState('');
  const [issueDescription, setReportIssueDescription] = useState('');
  const [issuePriority, setIssuePriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent'>('medium');
  const [issueMeterAtService, setIssueMeterAtService] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingIssue, setSubmittingIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueSuccess, setIssueSuccess] = useState(false);

  const scannerRef = useRef<Html5Qrcode | null>(null);
  const scannerId = "qr-reader-viewport";

  // Inicialização e Liberação da Câmera
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    // Reinicia os estados de ativo escaneado ao abrir
    setActiveAsset(null);
    setDocuments([]);
    setScannerError(null);
    setMeterValue('');
    setMeterSuccess(false);
    setReportIssue(false);
    setIssueTitle('');
    setReportIssueDescription('');
    setIssuePriority('medium');
    setIssueMeterAtService('');
    setIssueSuccess(false);

    // Pequeno atraso para garantir que o contêiner DOM esteja montado
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      startCamera();
    }, 400);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      stopCamera();
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  const startCamera = async () => {
    try {
      const html5Qrcode = new Html5Qrcode(scannerId);
      scannerRef.current = html5Qrcode;

      await html5Qrcode.start(
        { facingMode: "environment" },
        {
          fps: 10,
          qrbox: (width, height) => {
            const minEdge = Math.min(width, height);
            const boxSize = Math.floor(minEdge * 0.7);
            return { width: boxSize, height: boxSize };
          }
        },
        onScanSuccess,
        onScanFailure
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao iniciar câmera:", err);
      if (err.toString().includes("NotAllowedError") || err.toString().includes("Permission denied")) {
        setScannerError("Permissão de acesso à câmara foi negada. Por favor, ative as permissões nas configurações do navegador para escanear etiquetas.");
      } else {
        setScannerError("Não foi possível aceder à câmara traseira ou nenhuma câmara foi encontrada.");
      }
    }
  };

  const stopCamera = async () => {
    if (scannerRef.current && scannerRef.current.isScanning) {
      try {
        await scannerRef.current.stop();
        scannerRef.current.clear();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Erro ao parar câmera:", err);
      }
    }
    scannerRef.current = null;
  };

  const onScanSuccess = async (decodedText: string) => {
    // 1. Vibração tátil leve (se disponível)
    if (navigator.vibrate) {
      navigator.vibrate(100);
    }

    // 2. Para a câmera imediatamente para evitar múltiplos scans
    await stopCamera();

    // 3. Carrega o ativo escaneado
    loadAssetData(decodedText);
  };

  const onScanFailure = () => {
    // Ignoramos erros de frame individual para não poluir o console
  };

  const loadAssetData = async (assetId: string) => {
    setLoadingAsset(true);
    setScannerError(null);
    try {
      // Faz a requisição segura para obter o ativo pelo ID ou CodeTag
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${encodeURIComponent(assetId)}`);
      const asset = res.data?.asset || res.data?.data || res.data;
      
      if (!asset || !asset.id) {
        throw new Error("Equipamento ou viatura não cadastrada no CMMS.");
      }

      setActiveAsset(asset);
      setMeterValue(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssueMeterAtService(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssuePriority('medium');
      setIssueTitle('');
      
      // Carrega os documentos desse ativo
      loadAssetDocs(asset.id);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao buscar ativo escaneado:", err);
      setScannerError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 
        err.message || 
        "Código QR lido com sucesso, mas o ativo correspondente não foi encontrado na base de dados real."
      );
      // Reinicia câmera para tentar novamente
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (isOpen && !activeAsset) {
          startCamera();
        }
      }, 5000);
    } finally {
      setLoadingAsset(false);
    }
  };

  const loadAssetDocs = async (id: string) => {
    setLoadingDocs(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${id}/documents`);
      const docs = res.data?.documents || res.data || [];
      setDocuments(docs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Erro ao carregar documentos:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingDocs(false);
    }
  };

  // Enviar leitura do contador
  const handleUpdateMeter = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || meterValue === '') return;

    const current = activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0;
    if (Number(meterValue) < current) {
      alert(`O novo valor não pode ser menor do que o registo atual (${current}).`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingMeter(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/assets/${activeAsset.id}/meter`, {
        meterValue: Number(meterValue)
      });
      setMeterSuccess(true);
      
      // Atualiza o ativo local
      setActiveAsset(prev => {
        if (!prev) return null;
        return {
          ...prev,
          current_meter: Number(meterValue),
          currentMeter: Number(meterValue)
        };
      });
      
      setTimeout(() => setMeterSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao atualizar medidor:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao atualizar o contador no servidor.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingMeter(false);
    }
  };

  // Reportar Avaria (Criar Ordem de Serviço Corretiva)
  const handleReportIssueSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || !issueTitle.trim() || !issueDescription.trim() || issueMeterAtService === '') return;

    setSubmittingIssue(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        assetId: activeAsset.id,
        asset_id: activeAsset.id,
        title: issueTitle,
        description: issueDescription,
        orderType: 'corrective',
        order_type: 'corrective',
        priority: issuePriority,
        meterAtService: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        meter_at_service: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        status: 'open'
      };

      await api.post('/work-orders', payload);
      setIssueSuccess(true);
      setIssueTitle('');
      setReportIssueDescription('');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIssueSuccess(false);
        setReportIssue(false);
      }, 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao reportar avaria:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao criar ordem de serviço corretiva.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingIssue(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isVehicle = activeAsset && (activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_light' || activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_heavy');
  const level = activeAsset?.criticality_level || activeAsset?.criticalityLevel || 'B';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-lg rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* Header do Scanner */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <QrCode className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">Leitor de QR Code Móvel</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              stopCamera();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Corpo do Modal */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          
          {/* MODO A: ESCANEANDO (Sem ativo selecionado) */}
          {!activeAsset && !loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-6 space-y-6">
              
              {/* Moldura do Leitor QR */}
              <div className="relative w-72 h-72 border border-slate-300 rounded-md bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center">
                
                {/* Visualizador HTML5-QRCode */}
                <div id={scannerId} className="w-full h-full object-cover"></div>

                {/* Laser de Scan de Campo */}
                {!scannerError && (
                  <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-blue-600 shadow-md shadow-blue-500/50 animate-laser-scanning z-10 pointer-events-none"></div>
                )}

                {/* Cantos Iluminados (Moldura Tecnológica) */}
                <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tr-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-bl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-br-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
              </div>

              {/* Mensagem orientadora */}
              <div className="text-center px-4 max-w-sm">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                  Aponte a câmara do telemóvel para a etiqueta QR Code colada na viatura ou gerador.
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
                  Abertura automática de ficha técnica
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Tratamento de Erros de Permissão ou Scanner */}
              {scannerError && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start max-w-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha no Scanner</span>
                    <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{scannerError}</span>
                    <button
                      onClick={startCamera}
                      className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Tentar Novamente
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO B: CARREGANDO DETALHES DO ATIVO */}
          {loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 space-y-3">
              <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700" size={36} />
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium tracking-wide">A carregar detalhes de campo do ativo...</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO C: PAINEL DE AÇÃO RÁPIDA DE CAMPO (Ativo Identificado!) */}
          {activeAsset && (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-slide-up">
              
              {/* Header de Identificação do Ativo */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-xs text-blue-700 block tracking-wider uppercase">
                    {activeAsset.code_tag || activeAsset.codeTag}
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-base leading-tight">{activeAsset.name}</h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-600 block mt-0.5">{activeAsset.brand} {activeAsset.model}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium border ${
                    level === 'A' 
                      ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200' 
                      : level === 'B' 
                      ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' 
                      : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'
                  }`}>
                    Criticidade {level}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium uppercase">
                    {isVehicle ? "Frota de Campo" : "Equipamento Industrial"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Detalhes Técnicos e de Conformidade */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md space-y-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">
                  Ficha Operacional & Conformidade
                </span>
                
                {isVehicle ? (
                  /* INFORMAÇÕES DE VIATURA */
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Matrícula</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-mono font-bold">{activeAsset.license_plate || activeAsset.licensePlate || '---'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Odómetro Atual</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{(activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Validade Inspeção INATRO</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                        <Clock size={12} className="text-blue-700" />
                        {activeAsset.inatro_inspection_expiry || activeAsset.inatroInspectionExpiry 
                          ? new Date(activeAsset.inatro_inspection_expiry || activeAsset.inatroInspectionExpiry || '').toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ') 
                          : 'Pendente de Certificado'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  /* INFORMAÇÕES DE MAQUINARIA/GERADOR */
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Setor da Planta</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{activeAsset.plant_sector || activeAsset.plantSector || 'Não especificado'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Horímetro Atual</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{(activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} horas</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Tensão / Potência</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-mono font-bold">
                        {activeAsset.voltage_specs || activeAsset.voltageSpecs || '---'} / {activeAsset.power_kw_kva || activeAsset.powerKwKva || '---'} kW
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Calibração INNOQ</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Clock size={12} className="text-blue-700" />
                        {activeAsset.innoq_calibration_expiry || activeAsset.innoqCalibrationExpiry 
                          ? new Date(activeAsset.innoq_calibration_expiry || activeAsset.innoqCalibrationExpiry || '').toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ') 
                          : 'Sem calibração ativa'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO A: ATUALIZAR CONTADOR DE CAMPO */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Gauge size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>Atualizar Contador de Campo</span>
                </span>
                
                <form onSubmit={handleUpdateMeter} className="flex gap-2 items-end">
                  <div className="flex-1 relative">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0}
                      value={meterValue}
                      onChange={(e) => setMeterValue(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                    <span className="absolute right-3.5 top-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500">
                      {isVehicle ? "KM" : "HORAS"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submittingMeter || meterValue === '' || Number(meterValue) < (activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0)}
                    className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shrink-0 h-9"
                  >
                    {submittingMeter ? (
                      <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" />
                    ) : (
                      <span>Guardar</span>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </form>

                {meterSuccess && (
                  <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                    <span>Contador atualizado e sincronizado com sucesso!</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-1.5">
                  O valor atual é de {activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0} {isVehicle ? "km" : "horas"}. Não é possível inserir valores inferiores ao atual.
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO B: CONSULTAR DOCUMENTAÇÃO DE CAMPO */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <FileText size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>Documentos e Certificados do Ativo ({documents.length})</span>
                </span>

                {loadingDocs ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-4">
                    <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400 mx-auto" />
                  </div>
                ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">Nenhum certificado ou ficha anexada.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                    {documents.map((doc) => (
                      <div key={doc.id} className="p-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-between text-xs hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate mr-3">
                          <File size={14} className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" />
                          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900 truncate" title={doc.title}>{doc.title}</span>
                        </div>
                        <a
                          href={doc.signed_url || doc.signedUrl}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="text-xs text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline flex items-center gap-0.5 shrink-0"
                        >
                          <Eye size={12} />
                          <span>Abrir</span>
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO C: REPORTAR ANOMALIA / AVARIA */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                {!reportIssue ? (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setReportIssue(true)}
                    className="w-full py-2 bg-white hover:bg-red-50 border border-red-300 text-red-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Activity size={14} />
                    <span>Reportar Anomalia / Avaria na Viatura</span>
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <form onSubmit={handleReportIssueSubmit} className="space-y-3 animate-fade-in">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide block border-b border-slate-200 pb-2 mb-2">
                      Nova Ordem de Serviço (Avaria Corretiva)
                    </span>
                    
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Título da Avaria / Intervenção *</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={issueTitle}
                        onChange={(e) => setIssueTitle(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: Fuga de óleo no retentor ou motor com ruído"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Descrição detalhada do problema *</label>
                      <textarea
                        required
                        rows={2}
                        value={issueDescription}
                        onChange={(e) => setReportIssueDescription(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Descreva minuciosamente o problema técnico encontrado..."
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Contador no Momento ({isVehicle ? 'km' : 'horas'}) *</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          required
                          min={activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0}
                          value={issueMeterAtService}
                          onChange={(e) => setIssueMeterAtService(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Prioridade Sugerida *</label>
                        <select
                          value={issuePriority}
                          onChange={(e) => setIssuePriority(e.target.value as any)}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <option value="low">Baixa</option>
                          <option value="medium">Média</option>
                          <option value="high">Alta</option>
                          <option value="urgent">Urgente</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {issueSuccess && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 py-1">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                        <span>Ordem de Manutenção Corretiva Aberta!</span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setReportIssue(false)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md"
                      >
                        Cancelar
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={submittingIssue || !issueTitle.trim() || !issueDescription.trim() || issueMeterAtService === ''}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {submittingIssue ? (
                          <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" />
                        ) : (
                          <span>Abrir OS Corretiva</span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </form>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Botão para ler outro código */}
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setActiveAsset(null);
                  setDocuments([]);
                  startCamera();
                }}
                className="w-full py-2 border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 bg-white text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Escanear Outro Equipamento
              </button>

            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const useCurrentDomain = (): DomainConfig => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const scope = user?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operationalScope;
  const domain: 'fleet' | 'plant' = scope === 'plant' ? 'plant' : 'fleet';
  return DOMAIN_REGISTRY[domain];
};


// ===== src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx =====
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

export type UserRole = 'super_admin' | 'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator';

export interface User {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  role: UserRole;
  tenantName: string;
  tenantId: string;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both';
  tenant?: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    max_users?: number;
    maxUsers?: number;
    operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both';
    operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both';
  };
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<User>;
  setUser: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<User | null>>;
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
  isTenantAdmin: boolean;
  isSupervisor: boolean;
  isTechnician: boolean;
  isDriverOperator: boolean;
  isFleet: boolean;
  isPlant: boolean;
  currentScope: 'fleet' | 'plant';
  setCurrentScope: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<'fleet' | 'plant'>>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [currentScope, setCurrentScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant'>('fleet');

  const fetchProfile = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/auth/me');
      const userData = response.data?.user || response.data;
      if (userData && (userData.id || userData.email) && userData.role) {
        setUser(userData);
        // Inicializar o escopo padrão do utilizador
        const scope = userData.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operationalScope;
        if (scope === 'plant') {
          setCurrentScope('plant');
        } else {
          setCurrentScope('fleet');
        }
      } else {
        setUser(null);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProfile();

    const handleWindowFocus = () => {
      fetchProfile();
    };
    window.addEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      const scope = user.operational_scope || user.tenant?.operational_scope || user.tenant?.operationalScope;
      if (scope === 'plant') {
        setCurrentScope('plant');
      } else if (scope === 'fleet') {
        setCurrentScope('fleet');
      }
    }
  }, [user]);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string): Promise<User> => {
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password });
      
      const token = response.data?.token || response.data?.accessToken;
      if (token) {
        sessionStorage.setItem('cmms_auth_token', token);
        localStorage.setItem('cmms_auth_token', token);
      }

      const userData = response.data?.user || response.data;
      
      if (!userData || !userData.id || !userData.role) {
        throw new Error('Servidor retornou dados de utilizador inválidos.');
      }
      
      setUser(userData);
      const scope = userData.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operationalScope;
      if (scope === 'plant') {
        setCurrentScope('plant');
      } else {
        setCurrentScope('fleet');
      }

      return userData;
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errorMsg = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Erro ao efetuar login.';
      throw new Error(errorMsg);
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await api.post('/auth/logout').catch(() => {});
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao efetuar logout no servidor', err);
    } finally {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('cmms_auth_token');
      localStorage.removeItem('cmms_auth_token');
      setUser(null);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const isSuperAdmin = user?.role === 'super_admin';
  const isTenantAdmin = user?.role === 'tenant_admin';
  const isSupervisor = user?.role === 'supervisor';
  const isTechnician = user?.role === 'technician';
  const isDriverOperator = user?.role === 'driver_operator';

  const isFleet = currentScope === 'fleet';
  const isPlant = currentScope === 'plant';

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        loading,
        logout,
        login,
        setUser,
        isSuperAdmin,
        isTenantAdmin,
        isSupervisor,
        isTechnician,
        isDriverOperator,
        isFleet,
        isPlant,
        currentScope,
        setCurrentScope
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useAuth deve ser utilizado dentro de um AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};


// ===== src/hooks/useEscapeKey.ts =====
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export const useEscapeKey = (onClose: () => void, isOpen: boolean = true) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'Esc') {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);
};

export default useEscapeKey;


// ===== src/hooks/usePreventRefresh.ts =====
import { useEffect } from 'react';

/**
 * Hook para previnir o recarregamento ou encerramento acidental da página
 * no navegador (F5, fechar aba, fechar janela, botão recarregar).
 *
 * @param enabled Define se a proteção contra recarregamento está ativa (padrão: true)
 */
export const usePreventRefresh = (enabled: boolean = false) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    const handleBeforeUnload = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      // Cancela o evento conforme especificação
      event.preventDefault();
      // O Chrome requer que returnValue seja definido com uma string
      event.returnValue = '';
      return '';
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    };
  }, [enabled]);
};

export default usePreventRefresh;


// ===== src/hooks/useVersionCheck.ts =====
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export const useVersionCheck = () => {
  const [hasNewVersion, setHasNewVersion] = useState(false);

  const checkVersion = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/version.json?t=${Date.now()}`, {
        cache: 'no-store',
        headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }
      });
      if (!res.ok) return;

      const data = await res.json();
      const storedVersion = localStorage.getItem('cmms_app_version');

      if (!storedVersion) {
        localStorage.setItem('cmms_app_version', String(data.version));
      } else if (String(data.version) !== storedVersion) {
        setHasNewVersion(true);
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const updateApp = () => {
    if ('caches' in window) {
      caches.keys().then((names) => names.forEach((name) => caches.delete(name)));
    }
    localStorage.removeItem('cmms_app_version');
    window.location.reload();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    checkVersion();
    const interval = setInterval(checkVersion, 3 * 60 * 1000); // 3 minutos
    const onFocus = () => checkVersion();
    window.addEventListener('focus', onFocus);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      window.removeEventListener('focus', onFocus);
    };
  }, []);

  return { hasNewVersion, updateApp };
};

  const [insuranceCompany, setInsuranceCompany] = useState('');
  const [insurancePolicyNumber, setInsurancePolicyNumber] = useState('');
  const [insuranceExpiry, setInsuranceExpiry] = useState('');
  // Se Industrial (INNOQ e Teste Caldeira)
  const [innoqCalibrationExpiry, setInnoqCalibrationExpiry] = useState('');
  const [pressureTestExpiry, setPressureTestExpiry] = useState('');
  const [localSupplierName, setLocalSupplierName] = useState('');
  const [warrantyExpiry, setWarrantyExpiry] = useState('');

  // Etapa 4: Documento Inicial
  const [wizardFile, setWizardFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isDragActive, setIsDragActive] = useState(false);

  // ==========================================
  // ESTADOS DE DOCUMENTAÇÃO ADICIONAL
  // ==========================================
  const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [docType, setDocType] = useState('tech_sheet');
  const [docTitle, setDocTitle] = useState('');
  const [isUploadingDoc, setIsUploadingDoc] = useState(false);

  // ==========================================
  // BUSCA DE DADOS
  // ==========================================
  const fetchAssets = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/assets');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.assets || response.data?.data || [];
      setAssets(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao buscar ativos:', err);
      if (err.response?.status === 403) {
        setError('Acesso ao módulo não autorizado ou sessão expirada. Por favor, termine a sessão e entre novamente.');
      } else {
        setError(
          err.response?.data?.message ||
          err.message ||
          'Falha na comunicação com a API de ativos.'
        );
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAssets();
  }, []);

  // ==========================================
  // CARREGAR DOCUMENTOS DE UM ATIVO
  // ==========================================
  const loadDocuments = async (assetId: string) => {
    setDocLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/assets/${assetId}/documents`);
      const docs = response.data?.documents || response.data || [];
      setDocuments(docs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao obter documentos:', err);
    } finally {
      setDocLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // GERAÇÃO DE QR CODE (CANVAS)
  // ==========================================
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isQrModalOpen && selectedQrAsset && qrCanvasRef.current) {
      const valueToEncode = selectedQrAsset.id;
      QRCode.toCanvas(
        qrCanvasRef.current,
        valueToEncode,
        {
          width: 200,
          margin: 1.5,
          color: {
            dark: '#0f172a', // slate-900
            light: '#ffffff'
          }
        },
        (err) => {
          if (err) console.error('Erro ao gerar QR Code:', err);
        }
      );
    }
  }, [isQrModalOpen, selectedQrAsset]);

  // Download do QR Code como PNG
  const handleDownloadQrCode = () => {
    if (qrCanvasRef.current && selectedQrAsset) {
      const url = qrCanvasRef.current.toDataURL('image/png');
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `qrcode_ativo_${selectedQrAsset.code_tag || selectedQrAsset.id}.png`;
      link.click();
    }
  };

  // Imprimir Etiqueta
  const handlePrintLabel = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  // ==========================================
  // MANIPULAÇÃO DE ARQUIVOS (DRAG & DROP)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDrag = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.type === 'dragenter' || e.type === 'dragover') {
      setIsDragActive(true);
    } else if (e.type === 'dragleave') {
      setIsDragActive(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsDragActive(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
      if (file.size <= 20 * 1024 * 1024) {
        setWizardFile(file);
      } else {
        alert('Tamanho limite de ficheiro excedido (máximo 20MB).');
      }
    }
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (file.size <= 20 * 1024 * 1024) {
        setWizardFile(file);
      } else {
        alert('Tamanho limite de ficheiro excedido (máximo 20MB).');
      }
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // VALIDAÇÕES POR ETAPA
  // ==========================================
  const validateStep = (step: number): boolean => {
    if (step === 1) {
      if (!name.trim()) {
        alert('O Nome do Ativo é obrigatório.');
        return false;
      }
      if (!costCenter.trim()) {
        alert('O Centro de Custo/Província é obrigatório.');
        return false;
      }
      if (!model.trim()) {
        alert('O Modelo é obrigatório.');
        return false;
      }
    }
    // Etapa 2 e 3 são condicionais e possuem defaults, validação flexível de tipo
    return true;
  };

  const handleNextStep = () => {
    if (validateStep(currentStep)) {
      setCurrentStep((prev) => prev + 1);
    }
  };

  const handlePrevStep = () => {
    setCurrentStep((prev) => prev - 1);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // SUBMISSÃO MULTI-STEP (CRIAR E EDITAR)
  // ==========================================
  const handleSubmitForm = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!validateStep(1)) return;

    setSubmitting(true);

    const isVehicle = category === 'vehicle_light' || category === 'vehicle_heavy';
    const meterKind = isVehicle ? 'odometer_km' : 'hourmeter_hours';

    // Construção do Payload completo mapeado para garantir conformidade
    const payload: any = {
      name,
      category,
      codeTag,
      code_tag: codeTag || undefined,
      criticalityLevel,
      criticality_level: criticalityLevel,
      brand,
      model,
      manufactureYear: Number(manufactureYear),
      manufacture_year: Number(manufactureYear),
      costCenter,
      cost_center: costCenter,
      meterKind,
      meter_kind: meterKind,
      currentMeter: Number(currentMeter),
      current_meter: Number(currentMeter),
      status: modalMode === 'create' ? 'operational' : undefined, // padrão ao criar
    };

    if (isVehicle) {
      payload.licensePlate = licensePlate;
      payload.license_plate = licensePlate;
      payload.chassisVin = chassisVin;
      payload.chassis_vin = chassisVin;
      payload.engineNumber = engineNumber;
      payload.engine_number = engineNumber;
      payload.fuelTankCapacityLiters = fuelTankCapacityLiters ? Number(fuelTankCapacityLiters) : undefined;
      payload.fuel_tank_capacity_liters = fuelTankCapacityLiters ? Number(fuelTankCapacityLiters) : undefined;

      payload.inatroInspectionExpiry = inatroInspectionExpiry || undefined;
      payload.inatro_inspection_expiry = inatroInspectionExpiry || undefined;
      payload.insuranceCompany = insuranceCompany || undefined;
      payload.insurance_company = insuranceCompany || undefined;
      payload.insurancePolicyNumber = insurancePolicyNumber || undefined;
      payload.insurance_policy_number = insurancePolicyNumber || undefined;
      payload.insuranceExpiry = insuranceExpiry || undefined;
      payload.insurance_expiry = insuranceExpiry || undefined;
    } else {
      payload.plantSector = plantSector;
      payload.plant_sector = plantSector;
      payload.voltageSpecs = voltageSpecs;
      payload.voltage_specs = voltageSpecs;
      payload.powerKwKva = powerKwKva ? Number(powerKwKva) : undefined;
      payload.power_kw_kva = powerKwKva ? Number(powerKwKva) : undefined;

      payload.innoqCalibrationExpiry = innoqCalibrationExpiry || undefined;
      payload.innoq_calibration_expiry = innoqCalibrationExpiry || undefined;
      payload.pressureTestExpiry = pressureTestExpiry || undefined;
      payload.pressure_test_expiry = pressureTestExpiry || undefined;
      payload.localSupplierName = localSupplierName || undefined;
      payload.local_supplier_name = localSupplierName || undefined;
      payload.warrantyExpiry = warrantyExpiry || undefined;
      payload.warranty_expiry = warrantyExpiry || undefined;
    }

    try {
      let assetId = '';
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        // PASSO A: POST real
        const res = await api.post('/assets', payload);
        const createdAsset = res.data?.asset || res.data?.data || res.data;
        assetId = createdAsset.id;

        // PASSO B: Envio do ficheiro se selecionado
        if (assetId && wizardFile) {
          const formData = new FormData();
          formData.append('file', wizardFile);
          formData.append('documentType', 'tech_sheet');
          formData.append('title', 'Ficha Técnica Inicial');

          try {
            // NUNCA defina o cabeçalho 'Content-Type' manualmente em chamadas multipart
            await api.post(`/assets/${assetId}/documents`, formData);
          } catch (docErr) {
            console.error('Falha ao enviar documento inicial:', docErr);
            alert('Ativo cadastrado com sucesso, mas o upload da ficha falhou. Você pode reenviar o documento na lista.');
          }
        }
        setSuccessMessage('Ativo cadastrado com sucesso no CMMS!');
      } else {
        // EDIÇÃO: PUT real
        await api.put(`/assets/${editingAssetId}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Dados do Ativo atualizados com sucesso!');
      }

      // PASSO C: Fechar modal e recarregar
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      resetWizardForm();
      await fetchAssets();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar ativo:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao guardar dados do ativo.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const resetWizardForm = () => {
    setCurrentStep(1);
    setName('');
    setCategory((categories[0]?.value as any) || 'vehicle_light');
    setCodeTag('');
    setCriticalityLevel('B');
    setBrand('');
    setModel('');
    setManufactureYear(new Date().getFullYear());
    setCostCenter('');
    setInstallation('');
    setLicensePlate('');
    setChassisVin('');
    setEngineNumber('');
    setFuelTankCapacityLiters('');
    setPlantSector('');
    setVoltageSpecs('');
    setPowerKwKva('');
    setCurrentMeter(0);
    setInatroInspectionExpiry('');
    setInsuranceCompany('');
    setInsurancePolicyNumber('');
    setInsuranceExpiry('');
    setInnoqCalibrationExpiry('');
    setPressureTestExpiry('');
    setLocalSupplierName('');
    setWarrantyExpiry('');
    setWizardFile(null);
    setEditingAssetId(null);
  };

  // preenche formulário para edição
  const handleOpenEditModal = (asset: Asset) => {
    resetWizardForm();
    setModalOpenMode('edit');
    setEditingAssetId(asset.id);

    setCategory((asset.category as any) || 'vehicle_light');
    setName(asset.name || '');
    setCodeTag(asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || '');
    setCriticalityLevel((asset.criticality_level as any) || (asset.criticalityLevel as any) || 'B');
    setBrand(asset.brand || '');
    setModel(asset.model || '');
    setManufactureYear(asset.manufacture_year || asset.manufactureYear || new Date().getFullYear());
    setCostCenter(asset.cost_center || asset.costCenter || '');
    setInstallation((asset as any).installation || asset.plant_sector || asset.plantSector || '');

    // Etapa 2 e 3
    setLicensePlate(asset.license_plate || asset.licensePlate || '');
    setChassisVin(asset.chassis_vin || asset.chassisVin || '');
    setEngineNumber(asset.engine_number || asset.engineNumber || '');
    setFuelTankCapacityLiters(asset.fuel_tank_capacity_liters ?? asset.fuelTankCapacityLiters ?? '');
    setPlantSector(asset.plant_sector || asset.plantSector || '');
    setVoltageSpecs(asset.voltage_specs || asset.voltageSpecs || '');
    setPowerKwKva(asset.power_kw_kva ?? asset.powerKwKva ?? '');
    setCurrentMeter(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);

    setInatroInspectionExpiry(asset.inatro_inspection_expiry || asset.inatroInspectionExpiry || '');
    setInsuranceCompany(asset.insurance_company || asset.insuranceCompany || '');
    setInsurancePolicyNumber(asset.insurance_policy_number || asset.insurancePolicyNumber || '');
    setInsuranceExpiry(asset.insurance_expiry || asset.insuranceExpiry || '');

    setInnoqCalibrationExpiry(asset.innoq_calibration_expiry || asset.innoqCalibrationExpiry || '');
    setPressureTestExpiry(asset.pressure_test_expiry || asset.pressureTestExpiry || '');
    setLocalSupplierName(asset.local_supplier_name || asset.localSupplierName || '');
    setWarrantyExpiry(asset.warranty_expiry || asset.warrantyExpiry || '');

    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // ADICIONAR DOCUMENTAÇÃO COMPLEMENTAR
  // ==========================================
  const handleAddAdditionalDocument = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedDocAsset || !selectedFile || !docTitle.trim()) {
      alert('Preencha o título e selecione um documento.');
      return;
    }

    setIsUploadingDoc(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', selectedFile);
    formData.append('title', docTitle);
    formData.append('documentType', docType);

    try {
      // Execute o post diretamente com a instância api sem cabeçalho manual de Content-Type
      await api.post(`/assets/${selectedDocAsset.id}/documents`, formData);
      alert('Documento anexado com sucesso!');
      setDocTitle('');
      setSelectedFile(null);
      if (fileInputRef.current) {
        fileInputRef.current.value = '';
      }
      // atualize a listagem chamando api.get
      await loadDocuments(selectedDocAsset.id);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao fazer upload do documento:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao fazer upload do documento.');
    } finally {
      setIsUploadingDoc(false);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // SOFT-DELETE (ARQUIVAR ATIVO)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDeleteAsset = async () => {
    if (!deletingAsset) return;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/assets/${deletingAsset.id}`);
      setSuccessMessage(`Ativo "${deletingAsset.code_tag || deletingAsset.codeTag}" arquivado com sucesso.`);
      setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
      setDeletingAsset(null);
      await fetchAssets();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao arquivar ativo:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao arquivar ativo.');
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // FUNÇÕES DE IMPORTAÇÃO EM LOTE (CSV)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDownloadCsvTemplate = () => {
    let csvContent = '';
    if (currentScope === 'plant') {
      const plantCat1 = categories.length > 0 ? categories[0].value : 'generator';
      const plantCat2 = categories.length > 1 ? categories[1].value : 'machinery';
      csvContent = `Nome,Categoria,Tag,Fabricante,Modelo,Ano,SetorFabril,Potencia,Voltagem,HorimetroInicial\n` +
        `Gerador de Emergência 250kVA,${plantCat1},G-001,Caterpillar,CAT-250,2022,Subestação Principal,250,380V,1200\n` +
        `Compressor de Ar Comprimido,${plantCat2},C-002,Atlas Copco,GA37,2021,Linha de Produção 1,45,400V,3400`;
    } else {
      const fleetCat1 = categories.length > 0 ? categories[0].value : 'vehicle_light';
      const fleetCat2 = categories.length > 1 ? categories[1].value : 'vehicle_heavy';
      csvContent = `Nome,Categoria,Tag,Marca,Modelo,Ano,Matricula,Chassis,OdometroInicial\n` +
        `Camionete Hilux 2.8 4x4,${fleetCat1},V-001,Toyota,Hilux,2022,ABC-123-MC,CH123456789,15000\n` +
        `Camião Basculante 15T,${fleetCat2},V-002,Volvo,FMX 440,2020,AFG-890-MC,CH987654321,85000`;
    }

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `modelo_importacao_ativos_${currentScope || 'general'}.csv`);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  const parseCsvFile = (file: File) => {
    setBatchError(null);
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const text = e.target?.result as string;
      if (!text) {
        setBatchError('O ficheiro CSV selecionado está vazio.');
        setParsedBatchData([]);
        return;
      }

      const lines = text.split(/\r\n|\n/).filter((line) => line.trim().length > 0);
      if (lines.length < 2) {
        setBatchError('O ficheiro CSV deve conter a linha de cabeçalho e pelo menos um registo de ativo.');
        setParsedBatchData([]);
        return;
      }

      const firstLine = lines[0];
      const delimiter = firstLine.includes(';') ? ';' : ',';
      const headers = firstLine.split(delimiter).map((h) => h.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));

      const rows: any[] = [];
      let hasEmptyName = false;

      for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const currentLine = lines[i].trim();
        if (!currentLine) continue;

        const values = currentLine.split(delimiter).map((v) => v.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
        const rowObj: Record<string, string> = {};

        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
          rowObj[header] = values[index] || '';
        });

        const nomeValue = rowObj['Nome'] || rowObj['nome'] || rowObj['NOME'];
        if (!nomeValue || !nomeValue.trim()) {
          hasEmptyName = true;
        }

        rows.push(rowObj);
      }

      if (hasEmptyName) {
        setBatchError('Validação de Dados: Foram encontradas linhas sem o campo "Nome" preenchido. O campo Nome é obrigatório para todos os ativos.');
      }

      setParsedBatchData(rows);
    };

    reader.onerror = () => {
      setBatchError('Falha ao efetuar a leitura do ficheiro CSV.');
    };

    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  const handleBatchFileSelect = (file: File) => {
    if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.csv') && file.type !== 'text/csv' && file.type !== 'application/vnd.ms-excel') {
      setBatchError('Formato inválido. Por favor, carregue um ficheiro no formato CSV (.csv).');
      return;
    }
    setBatchFile(file);
    parseCsvFile(file);
  };

  const handleBatchDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(true);
  };

  const handleBatchDragLeave = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(false);
  };

  const handleBatchDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      handleBatchFileSelect(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleBatchSubmit = async () => {
    if (parsedBatchData.length === 0) {
      setBatchError('Nenhum registo válido encontrado no ficheiro CSV para importar.');
      return;
    }

    const invalidIndex = parsedBatchData.findIndex(
      row => !(row.Nome || row.nome || row.NOME)?.trim()
    );

    if (invalidIndex !== -1) {
      setBatchError(`A linha ${invalidIndex + 1} possui o campo "Nome" em branco. Corrija o ficheiro antes de importar.`);
      return;
    }

    setBatchSubmitting(true);
    setBatchError(null);

    const assetsToImport = parsedBatchData.map((row) => {
      const isVehicle = currentScope === 'fleet' || row.Categoria === 'vehicle_light' || row.Categoria === 'vehicle_heavy' || row.categoria === 'vehicle_light' || row.categoria === 'vehicle_heavy';
      const cat = row.Categoria || row.categoria || (isVehicle ? 'vehicle_light' : 'generator');
      const meterKind = isVehicle ? 'odometer_km' : 'hourmeter_hours';

      return {
        name: (row.Nome || row.nome || '').trim(),
        category: cat,
        codeTag: (row.Tag || row.tag || row.codeTag || row.code_tag || '').trim() || undefined,
        code_tag: (row.Tag || row.tag || row.codeTag || row.code_tag || '').trim() || undefined,
        brand: (row.Marca || row.marca || row.Fabricante || row.fabricante || '').trim() || undefined,
        model: (row.Modelo || row.modelo || '').trim() || '',
        manufactureYear: row.Ano ? Number(row.Ano) : new Date().getFullYear(),
        manufacture_year: row.Ano ? Number(row.Ano) : new Date().getFullYear(),
        costCenter: 'Sede / Geral',
        cost_center: 'Sede / Geral',
        meterKind,
        meter_kind: meterKind,
        currentMeter: Number(row.OdometroInicial || row.HorimetroInicial || row.odometroinicial || row.horimetroinicial || row.currentMeter || row.current_meter || 0),
        current_meter: Number(row.OdometroInicial || row.HorimetroInicial || row.odometroinicial || row.horimetroinicial || row.currentMeter || row.current_meter || 0),
        licensePlate: (row.Matricula || row.matricula || '').trim() || undefined,
        license_plate: (row.Matricula || row.matricula || '').trim() || undefined,
        chassisVin: (row.Chassis || row.chassis || '').trim() || undefined,
        chassis_vin: (row.Chassis || row.chassis || '').trim() || undefined,
        plantSector: (row.SetorFabril || row.setorfabril || '').trim() || undefined,
        plant_sector: (row.SetorFabril || row.setorfabril || '').trim() || undefined,
        powerKwKva: row.Potencia || row.potencia ? Number(row.Potencia || row.potencia) : undefined,
        power_kw_kva: row.Potencia || row.potencia ? Number(row.Potencia || row.potencia) : undefined,
        voltageSpecs: (row.Voltagem || row.voltagem || '').trim() || undefined,
        voltage_specs: (row.Voltagem || row.voltagem || '').trim() || undefined,
        status: 'operational'
      };
    });

    try {
      const response = await api.post('/assets/bulk', { assets: assetsToImport });

      const count = assetsToImport.length;
      setSuccessMessage(`${count} ${count === 1 ? 'ativo importado' : 'ativos importados'} em lote com sucesso!`);
      
      setIsBatchImportModalOpen(false);
      setBatchFile(null);
      setParsedBatchData([]);
      setBatchError(null);

      await fetchAssets();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao importar ativos em lote:', err);
      const errorDetail = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Falha ao processar a importação em lote dos ativos na API.';
      setBatchError(errorDetail);
    } finally {
      setBatchSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // AUXILIARES DE RENDERIZAÇÃO
  // ==========================================
  const formatCategory = (cat: string) => {
    switch (cat) {
      case 'vehicle_light':
        return 'Veículo Ligeiro';
      case 'vehicle_heavy':
        return 'Frota Pesada';
      case 'generator':
        return 'Gerador Industrial';
      case 'machinery':
        return 'Maquinaria / Chão Fábrica';
      default:
        return cat || 'Não especificado';
    }
  };

  const formatDocType = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'tech_sheet': return 'Ficha Técnica';
      case 'manual': return 'Manual Técnico';
      case 'diagram': return 'Diagrama Elétrico / Mecânico';
      case 'inspection_certificate': return 'Certificado de Inspeção (INATRO/INNOQ)';
      case 'insurance': return 'Apólice de Seguro';
      case 'asset_photo': return 'Fotografia do Ativo';
      default: return type;
    }
  };

  const renderStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'operational':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
            Disponível
          </span>
        );
      case 'breakdown':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200">
            Avariado
          </span>
        );
      case 'maintenance':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
            Em Oficina
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
            {status}
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  // Filtragem dos ativos localmente
  const filteredAssets = assets.filter((asset) => {
    const code = (asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || '').toLowerCase();
    const assetName = (asset.name || '').toLowerCase();
    const assetModel = (asset.model || '').toLowerCase();
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();

    const matchesSearch = code.includes(query) || assetName.includes(query) || assetModel.includes(query);
    const matchesCategory = categoryFilter === 'all' || asset.category === categoryFilter;

    // Filtro baseado no escopo ativo ('fleet' ou 'plant')
    const isAssetPlantType = asset.category === 'generator' || asset.category === 'machinery';
    const isAssetFleetType = asset.category === 'vehicle_light' || asset.category === 'vehicle_heavy';
    
    let matchesScope = true;
    if (currentScope === 'plant') {
      matchesScope = isAssetPlantType;
    } else if (currentScope === 'fleet') {
      matchesScope = isAssetFleetType;
    }

    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory && matchesScope;
  });

  // Se for driver_operator, redireciona para a interface simplificada tátil
  if (isDriverOperator) {
    return (
      <DriverOperatorPortal assets={assets} fetchAssets={fetchAssets} loading={loading} />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 print:hidden">
      
      {/* Toast de Sucesso */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Sucesso</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Barra de Ações */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
            <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />
            <span>A carregar...</span>
          </div>
        ) : <div />}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={fetchAssets}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
          </button>
          {!isTechnician && !isDriverOperator && (
            <>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setBatchFile(null);
                  setParsedBatchData([]);
                  setBatchError(null);
                  setIsBatchImportModalOpen(true);
                }}
                className="bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
              >
                <FileSpreadsheet size={16} className="text-slate-500" />
                <span>Importar em Lote</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  resetWizardForm();
                  setModalOpenMode('create');
                  setIsModalOpen(true);
                }}
                className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
              >
                <Plus size={16} />
                <span>Cadastrar Ativo</span>
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tratamento de erro */}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start">
          <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="flex-1">
            <span className="font-bold block text-red-800 mb-0.5">Erro na API</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{error}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Caixa de Busca e Filtros */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <select
            value={categoryFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-700 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas as Categorias</option>
            {categories.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                {c.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Ativos com Ações Completas */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded w-1/4 animate-pulse"></div>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="flex gap-4 items-center py-2">
                <div className="h-8 bg-slate-100 rounded flex-1 animate-pulse"></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filteredAssets.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 px-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhum Ativo Encontrado</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
              {(user?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operational_scope || domain) === 'plant' 
                ? 'Registe equipamentos fabris para iniciar.' 
                : 'Registe viaturas e frotas para iniciar.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs uppercase bg-slate-50">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Código / Tag</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Ativo / Equipamento</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Categoria</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Centro de Custo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Criticidade</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredAssets.map((asset) => {
                  const tag = asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || 'AUTO-TAG';
                  const level = asset.criticality_level || asset.criticalityLevel || 'B';
                  return (
                    <tr key={asset.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-blue-700">{tag}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-col">
                          <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium text-sm">{asset.name}</span>
                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{asset.brand} {asset.model}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-slate-600">
                        {formatCategory(asset.category)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-slate-600">
                        {asset.cost_center || asset.costCenter}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                        <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium border ${
                          level === 'A' 
                            ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200' 
                            : level === 'B' 
                            ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' 
                            : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'
                        }`}>
                          Nível {level}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                        {renderStatusBadge(asset.status)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                          {/* Gestão Documental */}
                          <button
                            onClick={() => {
                              setSelectedDocAsset(asset);
                              setIsDocModalOpen(true);
                              loadDocuments(asset.id);
                            }}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-colors"
                            title="Documentos do Ativo"
                          >
                            <FileText size={15} />
                          </button>
                          
                          {/* QR Code */}
                          <button
                            onClick={() => {
                              setSelectedQrAsset(asset);
                              setIsQrModalOpen(true);
                            }}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-colors"
                            title="Código QR / Etiqueta"
                          >
                            <QrCode size={15} />
                          </button>

                          {/* Editar */}
                          {!isTechnician && !isDriverOperator && (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(asset)}
                              className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-colors"
                              title="Editar Ativo"
                            >
                              <Edit2 size={15} />
                            </button>
                          )}

                          {/* Arquivar / Delete */}
                          {!isTechnician && !isDriverOperator && (
                            <button
                              onClick={() => {
                                setDeletingAsset(asset);
                                setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
                              }}
                              className="p-1.5 text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-md transition-colors"
                              title="Arquivar Ativo"
                            >
                              <Trash2 size={15} />
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ==========================================
          MODAL: ASSISTENTE DE CADASTRO (4 ETAPAS)
          ========================================== */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-xl rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Wrench className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {modalMode === 'create' ? 'Cadastrar Novo Ativo' : 'Editar Ativo Corporativo'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Indicador de Passos */}
            <div className="bg-slate-50/60 px-6 py-3 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border font-mono text-[11px] ${
                  currentStep >= 1 ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-600 text-blue-700 font-bold' : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-400'
                }`}>1</span>
                <span className={currentStep >= 1 ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-400'}>Identificação</span>
              </div>
              <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border font-mono text-[11px] ${
                  currentStep >= 2 ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-600 text-blue-700 font-bold' : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-400'
                }`}>2</span>
                <span className={currentStep >= 2 ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-400'}>Operação</span>
              </div>
              <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border font-mono text-[11px] ${
                  currentStep >= 3 ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-600 text-blue-700 font-bold' : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-400'
                }`}>3</span>
                <span className={currentStep >= 3 ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-400'}>Conformidade</span>
              </div>
              {modalMode === 'create' && (
                <>
                  <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border font-mono text-[11px] ${
                      currentStep >= 4 ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-600 text-blue-700 font-bold' : 'border-slate-300 text-slate-400'
                    }`}>4</span>
                    <span className={currentStep >= 4 ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-400'}>Anexos</span>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Formulário Wizard */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmitForm} className="p-6 space-y-4 flex-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[60vh] scrollbar-thin">
              
              {/* ETAPA 1: IDENTIFICAÇÃO */}
              {currentStep === 1 && (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                        Categoria de Ativo *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={category}
                        disabled={modalMode === 'edit'} // Categoria fixa na edição
                        onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {categories.map((c) => (
                          <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                            {c.label}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                        Tag / Código do Ativo
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={codeTag}
                        onChange={(e) => setCodeTag(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Deixe vazio para gerar automaticamente"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
