          src="https://i.postimg.cc/NfvHTQTR/avexas-logo-transp-copy.png"
          alt="Avexas CMMS"
          className="h-10 w-auto object-contain"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Cartão do Formulário Compacto */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm w-full max-w-sm p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 z-10">
        
        {/* Banner de Alerta de Erro */}
        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-lg flex gap-2.5 items-start animate-fade-in">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="shrink-0 text-red-600 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha na Autenticação</span>
              <span className="leading-relaxed text-red-700">{error}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              E-mail
            </label>
            <input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              required
              disabled={loading}
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Senha
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                id="password"
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                required
                disabled={loading}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                onKeyUp={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                className="w-full h-10 pl-3 pr-10 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                aria-label={showPassword ? 'Ocultar senha' : 'Exibir senha'}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
              </button>
            </div>
            {capsLockActive && (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-600 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                ⇪ Fixador de Maiúsculas (Caps Lock) ativado
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-center my-2">
            Acesso restrito a utilizadores autorizados
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full h-10 bg-[#0066FF] hover:bg-[#0052CC] text-white font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <RefreshCw size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                <span>Acessar...</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <span>Acessar</span>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LoginPage;


// ===== src/pages/SuperAdmin.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  Plus,
  Building2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  User,
  Shield,
  Layers,
  Edit2,
  Users,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Trash2
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Tenant {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trade_name?: string;
  tradeName?: string;
  nuit: string;
  province: string;
  max_assets_limit?: number;
  maxAssetsLimit?: number;
  max_users_limit?: number;
  maxUsersLimit?: number;
  max_users?: number;
  maxUsers?: number;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed' | string;
  operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed' | string;
  is_active?: boolean;
  isActive?: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
}

export const SuperAdminPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [tenants, setTenants] = useState<Tenant[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Estados de Controle de Modal e Submissão
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalMode, setModalMode] = useState<'create' | 'edit'>('create');
  const [editingTenantId, setEditingTenantId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal de Exclusão/Arquivamento
  const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deletingTenant, setDeletingTenant] = useState<Tenant | null>(null);

  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false), isDeleteModalOpen);

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAdminPassword, setShowAdminPassword] = useState(false);

  // Campos do Formulário de Registro/Edição de Empresa
  const [tenantName, setTenantName] = useState('');
  const [tradeName, setTradeName] = useState('');
  const [nuit, setNuit] = useState('');
  const [province, setProvince] = useState('');
  const [maxAssetsLimit, setMaxAssetsLimit] = useState<number>(50);
  const [maxUsersLimit, setMaxUsersLimit] = useState<number>(15);
  const [operationalScope, setOperationalScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed'>('mixed');

  // Campos do Administrador Inicial (apenas no cadastro)
  const [adminFullName, setAdminFullName] = useState('');
  const [adminEmail, setAdminEmail] = useState('');
  const [adminPassword, setAdminPassword] = useState('');
  const [adminPhone, setAdminPhone] = useState('');

  // Busca lista de empresas/tenants
  const fetchTenants = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      let data = [];
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      } catch {
        const response = await api.get('/super-admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      }
      setTenants(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar empresas:', err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.message ||
        'Não foi possível ligar ao servidor para carregar a lista de empresas.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTenants();
  }, []);

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    setModalMode('create');
    setEditingTenantId(null);
    setTenantName('');
    setTradeName('');
    setNuit('');
    setProvince('');
    setMaxAssetsLimit(50);
    setMaxUsersLimit(15);
    setOperationalScope('mixed');
    setAdminFullName('');
    setAdminEmail('');
    setAdminPassword('');
    setAdminPhone('');
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (tenant: Tenant) => {
    setModalMode('edit');
    setEditingTenantId(tenant.id);
    setTenantName(tenant.name || '');
    setTradeName(tenant.trade_name || tenant.tradeName || '');
    setNuit(tenant.nuit || '');
    setProvince(tenant.province || '');
    setMaxAssetsLimit(tenant.max_assets_limit ?? tenant.maxAssetsLimit ?? 50);
    setMaxUsersLimit(tenant.max_users_limit ?? tenant.max_users ?? tenant.maxUsers ?? 15);
    setOperationalScope((tenant.operational_scope || tenant.operationalScope || 'mixed') as any);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!tenantName || !tradeName || !nuit || !province) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha todos os campos obrigatórios da empresa (*).');
      return;
    }

    if (modalMode === 'create' && (!adminFullName || !adminEmail || !adminPassword)) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha os dados do utilizador administrador inicial.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        const createPayload = {
          tenantName,
          tenant_name: tenantName,
          tradeName,
          trade_name: tradeName,
          nuit,
          province,
          maxAssetsLimit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
          max_assets_limit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
          maxUsersLimit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
          max_users_limit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
          operationalScope,
          operational_scope: operationalScope,
          adminFullName,
          admin_full_name: adminFullName,
          adminEmail,
          admin_email: adminEmail,
          adminPassword,
          admin_password: adminPassword,
          adminPhone,
          admin_phone: adminPhone
        };

        try {
          await api.post('/admin/tenants', createPayload);
        } catch {
          await api.post('/super-admin/tenants', createPayload);
        }

        setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${tenantName}" registada e administrador configurado com sucesso!`);
      } else {
        const editPayload = {
          name: tenantName,
          tenantName,
          tenant_name: tenantName,
          tradeName,
          trade_name: tradeName,
          nuit,
          province,
          maxAssetsLimit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
          max_assets_limit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
          maxUsersLimit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
          max_users_limit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
          operationalScope,
          operational_scope: operationalScope
        };

        try {
          await api.put(`/admin/tenants/${editingTenantId}`, editPayload);
        } catch {
          await api.put(`/super-admin/tenants/${editingTenantId}`, editPayload);
        }

        setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${tenantName}" e parâmetros contratuais atualizados com sucesso!`);
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      await fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar empresa:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao guardar dados da empresa.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // Alternar Estado (Suspender / Ativar)
  const handleToggleTenantStatus = async (tenant: Tenant) => {
    const currentActive = tenant.is_active ?? tenant.isActive ?? true;
    const nextActive = !currentActive;

    try {
      try {
        await api.patch(`/admin/tenants/${tenant.id}/status`, { is_active: nextActive, isActive: nextActive });
      } catch {
        await api.patch(`/super-admin/tenants/${tenant.id}/status`, { is_active: nextActive, isActive: nextActive });
      }

      setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${tenant.name}" ${nextActive ? 'ativada' : 'suspensa'} com sucesso!`);
      await fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao alterar estado da empresa:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao alterar o estado da empresa.');
    }
  };

  // Confirmar Exclusão/Arquivamento do Contrato
  const handleConfirmDelete = (tenant: Tenant) => {
    setDeletingTenant(tenant);
    setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteTenant = async () => {
    if (!deletingTenant) return;

    try {
      try {
        await api.delete(`/admin/tenants/${deletingTenant.id}`);
      } catch {
        await api.delete(`/super-admin/tenants/${deletingTenant.id}`);
      }

      setSuccessMessage(`Contrato da empresa "${deletingTenant.name}" cancelado e arquivado com sucesso.`);
      setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
      setDeletingTenant(null);
      await fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao arquivar empresa:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao arquivar empresa.');
    }
  };

  // Métricas rápidas calculadas
  const totalTenants = tenants.length;
  const activeTenants = tenants.filter(t => t.is_active ?? t.isActive ?? true).length;
  const totalAssetsSum = tenants.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.max_assets_limit ?? t.maxAssetsLimit ?? 0), 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* Toast de Sucesso */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Operação Concluída</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Cabeçalho */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>Gestão de Empresas & Contratos</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Painel administrativo global de controlo de licenças, NUITs e parâmetros contratuais em Moçambique.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={fetchTenants}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Recarregar empresas"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Registar Nova Empresa</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Alerta de erro da API */}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start">
          <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="flex-1">
            <span className="font-bold block text-red-800 mb-0.5">Erro de Administração Global</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{error}</span>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={fetchTenants}
            className="text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 transition-colors"
          >
            Tentar Novamente
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid de Métricas Globais Reais */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Total de Empresas</span>
          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
            {loading ? '---' : totalTenants}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Sistemas industriais ativos</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Delegações Ativas</span>
          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
            {loading ? '---' : activeTenants}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Clientes com licenças válidas</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Limite Total de Ativos</span>
          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
            {loading ? '---' : totalAssetsSum}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Capacidade contratada global</span>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Taxa de Operação</span>
          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
            {loading ? '---' : '100%'}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">SLA do Servidor CMMS Cloud</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Empresas Contratantes */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
          <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-900">Empresas Contratantes</span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">Total de {tenants.length} registadas</span>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          /* SKELETON */
          <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded w-1/4 animate-pulse"></div>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="flex gap-4 items-center py-2.5">
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-12 animate-pulse"></div>
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded flex-1 animate-pulse"></div>
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-24 animate-pulse"></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : tenants.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 px-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border border-slate-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Building2 size={20} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhuma Empresa Cadastrada</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
              Utilize o botão acima para registar e provisionar o primeiro cliente corporativo no CMMS.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-50">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Nome da Empresa</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Nome Comercial</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">NUIT</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Província</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Âmbito Operacional</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Limites (Ativos / Users)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {tenants.map((tenant) => {
                  const companyName = tenant.name;
                  const trade = tenant.trade_name || tenant.tradeName || '---';
                  const active = tenant.is_active ?? tenant.isActive ?? true;
                  const limitAssets = tenant.max_assets_limit ?? tenant.maxAssetsLimit ?? 50;
                  const limitUsers = tenant.max_users_limit ?? tenant.max_users ?? tenant.maxUsers ?? 15;

                  return (
                    <tr key={tenant.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                        {companyName}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">
                        {trade}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-600">
                        {tenant.nuit}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                        {tenant.province}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                        {(() => {
                          const scope = tenant.operational_scope || tenant.operationalScope || 'fleet';
                          if (scope === 'fleet') {
                            return (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
                                Frota
                              </span>
                            );
                          } else if (scope === 'plant') {
                            return (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300">
                                Fabril
                              </span>
                            );
                          } else {
                            return (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200">
                                Frota e Fabril
                              </span>
                            );
                          }
                        })()}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-blue-700 font-bold">
                        {limitAssets} ativos / {limitUsers} users
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium border ${
                          active
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                            : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'
                        }`}>
                          {active ? 'Ativo' : 'Suspenso'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right space-x-1.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(tenant)}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          title="Editar Empresa & Parâmetros"
                        >
                          <Edit2 size={13} />
                        </button>
                        
                        <button
                          onClick={() => navigate(`/app/users?tenant_id=${tenant.id}`)}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-800 rounded-md border border-slate-300 hover:border-blue-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          title="Gerir Utilizadores da Empresa"
                        >
                          <Users size={13} />
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleToggleTenantStatus(tenant)}
                          className={`p-1.5 rounded-md border transition-colors cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${
                            active
                              ? 'bg-white hover:bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-800 border-slate-300 hover:border-amber-200'
                              : 'bg-white hover:bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:text-emerald-800 border-slate-300 hover:border-emerald-200'
                          }`}
                          title={active ? "Suspender Empresa" : "Ativar Empresa"}
                        >
                          {active ? <Pause size={13} /> : <Play size={13} />}
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleConfirmDelete(tenant)}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 rounded-md border border-slate-300 hover:border-red-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          title="Arquivar / Cancelar Contrato"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={13} />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MODAL DE CADASTRO / EDIÇÃO DE EMPRESA */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-xl rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Building2 className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {modalMode === 'create' ? 'Registar Nova Empresa' : 'Editar Empresa & Parâmetros Contratuais'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-5 max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
              
              {/* Seção 1: Dados da Empresa */}
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Layers size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>1. Informações Corporativas</span>
                </h3>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="tenantName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Nome Oficial / Razão Social *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="tenantName"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={tenantName}
                      onChange={(e) => setTenantName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Caminhos de Ferro, Lda"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="tradeName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Nome Comercial / Fantasia *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="tradeName"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={tradeName}
                      onChange={(e) => setTradeName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: CFM Logística"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-3">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="nuit" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      NUIT (Identificação Fiscal) *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="nuit"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={nuit}
                      onChange={(e) => setNuit(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: 400123456"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="province" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Província Sede *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="province"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={province}
                      onChange={(e) => setProvince(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Maputo, Sofala"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 mt-3">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="operationalScope" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Âmbito Operacional Contratado *
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="operationalScope"
                      required
                      value={operationalScope}
                      onChange={(e) => setOperationalScope(e.target.value as 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed')}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <option value="fleet">Frota (Transportes/Logística)</option>
                      <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril (Indústria/Maquinaria)</option>
                      <option value="mixed">Operação Mista (Frota + Fábrica)</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="maxAssets" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Limite de Ativos *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="maxAssets"
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={1}
                      value={maxAssetsLimit}
                      onChange={(e) => setMaxAssetsLimit(Number(e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="maxUsers" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Limite de Utilizadores *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="maxUsers"
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={1}
                      value={maxUsersLimit}
                      onChange={(e) => setMaxUsersLimit(Number(e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Seção 2: Administrador Inicial (Apenas no cadastro de nova empresa) */}
              {modalMode === 'create' && (
                <div className="border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <User size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                    <span>2. Utilizador Administrador Inicial</span>
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Nome Completo *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminName"
                        type="text"
                        required={modalMode === 'create'}
                        value={adminFullName}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminFullName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: João Sitoe"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminPhone" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Telefone / Celular
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminPhone"
                        type="text"
                        value={adminPhone}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminPhone(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: +258 84 123 4567"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-3">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminEmail" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        E-mail Corporativo *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminEmail"
                        type="email"
                        required={modalMode === 'create'}
                        value={adminEmail}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminEmail(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: admin@empresa.co.mz"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminPass" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Palavra-passe de Acesso *
                      </label>
                      <div className="relative">
                        <input
                          id="adminPass"
                          type={showAdminPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                          required={modalMode === 'create'}
                          minLength={8}
                          value={adminPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setAdminPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Mínimo 8 caracteres"
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-3 pr-10 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setShowAdminPassword(!showAdminPassword)}
                          className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {showAdminPassword ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Botões do Modal */}
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 mt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {submitting ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                      <span>{modalMode === 'create' ? 'A provisionar...' : 'A guardar...'}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span>{modalMode === 'create' ? 'Registar Empresa' : 'Guardar Alterações'}</span>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL DE CONFIRMAÇÃO DE CANCELAMENTO / ARQUIVAMENTO */}
      {isDeleteModalOpen && deletingTenant && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-sm shadow-xl p-6 text-center animate-slide-up">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <AlertTriangle size={24} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Cancelar Contrato da Empresa?</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-6">
              Tem a certeza de que deseja suspender/arquivar o contrato da empresa <strong className="text-slate-900">{deletingTenant.name}</strong>?
              Esta ação afetará o acesso dos utilizadores associados.
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-3 justify-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleDeleteTenant}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Sim, Arquivar
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default SuperAdminPage;


// ===== src/pages/Users.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  Plus,
  Users as UsersIcon,
  Search,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Shield,
  Briefcase,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  Mail,
  User as UserIcon,
  Layers,
  Building2,
  ArrowLeft
} from 'lucide-react';

interface TenantUser {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  fullName_?: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator';
  operational_scope: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both';
  operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both';
  specialty?: string;
  created_at?: string;
}

interface TenantItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trade_name?: string;
  tradeName?: string;
  max_users_limit?: number;
  max_users?: number;
  maxUsers?: number;
  operational_scope?: string;
}

export const UsersPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, isSuperAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { technicianSpecialties } = useCurrentDomain();

  const [tenants, setTenants] = useState<TenantItem[]>([]);
  const [selectedTenantId, setSelectedTenantId] = useState<string>('');
  const [loadingTenants, setLoadingTenants] = useState<boolean>(true);

  const [users, setUsers] = useState<TenantUser[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Modais
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalMode, setModalMode] = useState<'create' | 'edit'>('create');
  const [editingUserId, setEditingUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deletingUser, setDeletingUser] = useState<TenantUser | null>(null);

  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false), isDeleteModalOpen);

  // Campos do Formulário
  const [modalTenantId, setModalTenantId] = useState<string>('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator'>('technician');
  const [operationalScope, setOperationalScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both'>('fleet');
  const [specialty, setSpecialty] = useState('');

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Tenant atualmente selecionado para cálculo de licenças
  const selectedTenant = tenants.find((t) => t.id === (selectedTenantId || modalTenantId));
  const maxUsersLimit = selectedTenant?.max_users_limit || selectedTenant?.max_users || selectedTenant?.maxUsers || 15;

  // Carrega lista de empresas/tenants (Super Admin)
  const fetchTenants = async () => {
    setLoadingTenants(true);
    try {
      let data = [];
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      } catch {
        const response = await api.get('/super-admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      }
      setTenants(data);
      if (data.length > 0 && !selectedTenantId) {
        setSelectedTenantId(data[0].id);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar lista de empresas:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTenants(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega utilizadores filtrados por empresa
  const fetchUsers = async (tenantIdToFetch?: string) => {
    const targetTenantId = tenantIdToFetch !== undefined ? tenantIdToFetch : selectedTenantId;
    if (!targetTenantId) {
      setUsers([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/users?tenant_id=${targetTenantId}`);
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.users || response.data?.data || [];

      // Adaptando nomes dos atributos vindos do backend e filtrando defensivamente is_active === false
      const activeData = data.filter((u: any) => u.is_active !== false && u.isActive !== false);

      const normalizedUsers = activeData.map((u: any) => ({
        id: u.id,
        fullName: u.fullName || u.fullName_ || u.full_name || u.name || 'Utilizador Técnico',
        email: u.email,
        role: u.role,
        operational_scope: u.operational_scope || u.operationalScope || 'fleet',
        specialty: u.specialty || u.technical_specialty || u.specialization || '',
        is_active: u.is_active ?? u.isActive ?? true
      }));

      setUsers(normalizedUsers.filter((u: any) => u.is_active !== false));
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar utilizadores:', err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.message ||
        'Não foi possível ligar ao servidor para carregar a lista de utilizadores da empresa selecionada.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTenants();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedTenantId) {
      fetchUsers(selectedTenantId);
    } else {
      setUsers([]);
    }
  }, [selectedTenantId]);

  if (!isSuperAdmin && user?.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-8 text-center max-w-md mx-auto my-12 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-950/40 text-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 border border-red-800/30">
          <Shield size={24} />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-md font-bold text-slate-100">Acesso Restrito ao Super Admin</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          Apenas Administradores Globais (Super Admin) têm permissão para gerir os utilizadores de empresas e contratos.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    if (!selectedTenantId && tenants.length > 0) {
      setModalTenantId(tenants[0].id);
    } else {
      setModalTenantId(selectedTenantId);
    }
    setModalMode('create');
    setEditingUserId(null);
    setFullName('');
    setEmail('');
    setPassword('');
    setRole('technician');
    setOperationalScope('fleet');
    setSpecialty(technicianSpecialties.length > 0 ? technicianSpecialties[0].value : '');
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (targetUser: TenantUser) => {
    const targetScope = targetUser.operational_scope === 'plant' ? 'plant' : 'fleet';
    setModalMode('edit');
    setEditingUserId(targetUser.id);
    setFullName(targetUser.fullName);
    setEmail(targetUser.email);
    setPassword('');
    setRole(targetUser.role);
    setOperationalScope(targetScope);
    setSpecialty(targetUser.specialty || (technicianSpecialties.length > 0 ? technicianSpecialties[0].value : ''));
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!fullName.trim() || !email.trim()) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha os campos obrigatórios (*).');
      return;
    }

    const activeTenantId = selectedTenantId || modalTenantId;
    if (modalMode === 'create' && !activeTenantId) {
      alert('Por favor, selecione uma empresa (tenant) antes de criar o utilizador.');
      return;
    }

    if (modalMode === 'create' && !password.trim()) {
      alert('A palavra-passe é obrigatória para novos utilizadores.');
      return;
    }
    if (role === 'technician' && !specialty) {
      alert('Por favor, selecione a especialidade técnica.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        if (users.length >= maxUsersLimit) {
          alert(`Limite de utilizadores atingido para esta empresa (${maxUsersLimit}). Não é possível criar mais contas.`);
          setSubmitting(false);
          return;
        }

        const createPayload = {
          tenant_id: activeTenantId,
          tenantId: activeTenantId,
          full_name: fullName.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          password: password,
          role: role,
          operational_scope: operationalScope,
          operationalScope: operationalScope,
          ...(role === 'technician' && {
            specialty: specialty,
            technical_specialty: specialty,
            specialization: specialty
          })
        };

        await api.post('/users', createPayload);
        setSuccessMessage('Utilizador criado com sucesso!');
      } else {
        const editPayload = {
          name: fullName.trim(),
          full_name: fullName.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          role: role,
          operational_scope: operationalScope,
          operationalScope: operationalScope,
          ...(role === 'technician' && {
            specialty: specialty,
            technical_specialty: specialty,
            specialization: specialty
          }),
          ...(password.trim() ? { password } : {})
        };

        await api.put(`/users/${editingUserId}`, editPayload);
        setSuccessMessage('Utilizador atualizado com sucesso!');
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      await fetchUsers(activeTenantId);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar utilizador:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Erro ao gravar utilizador.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmDelete = (targetUser: TenantUser) => {
    setDeletingUser(targetUser);
    setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteUser = async () => {
    if (!deletingUser) return;
    const targetUserId = deletingUser.id;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/users/${targetUserId}`);
      setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((user) => user.id !== targetUserId));
      setSuccessMessage('Utilizador removido do contrato com sucesso.');
      setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
      setDeletingUser(null);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao eliminar utilizador:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao eliminar utilizador.');
    }
  };

  // Filtragem local de busca
  const filteredUsers = users.filter((u) => {
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return (
      u.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      u.email.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      u.role.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );
  });

  const getRoleBadgeColor = (userRole: string) => {
    switch (userRole) {
      case 'tenant_admin':
        return 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200';
      case 'technician':
        return 'bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-700 border border-cyan-200';
      case 'driver_operator':
        return 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200';
    }
  };

  const formatRoleName = (userRole: string) => {
    switch (userRole) {
      case 'tenant_admin':
        return 'Gestor de Contrato (Admin)';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'Supervisor Operacional';
      case 'technician':
        return 'Técnico de Manutenção';
      case 'driver_operator':
        return 'Motorista / Operador';
      default:
        return userRole;
    }
  };

  const formatScopeName = (scope: string) => {
    switch (scope) {
      case 'fleet':
        return 'Frota';
      case 'plant':
        return 'Instalação Fabril';
      default:
        return 'Frota';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
            className="p-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            title="Voltar"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={18} />
          </button>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <UsersIcon className="text-blue-700" size={24} />
              Gestão de Utilizadores & Técnicos
            </h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
              Selecione uma empresa para gerir as contas e atribuir níveis de acesso de supervisores e técnicos.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => selectedTenantId && fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold"
            title="Atualizar lista"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
            <span>Atualizar</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Registar Novo Utilizador / Técnico</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SELETOR DE EMPRESA (TENANT) NO TOPO */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-none">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
          <Building2 className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" size={20} />
          <label htmlFor="tenant-select" className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">
            Empresa:
          </label>
          {loadingTenants ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={14} />
              <span>A carregar empresas...</span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <select
              id="tenant-select"
              value={selectedTenantId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedTenantId(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full md:w-72 bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="">[ Selecione uma Empresa ▾ ]</option>
              {tenants.map((t) => (
                <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                  {t.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* INDICADOR DE UTILIZADORES USADOS DO TENANT SELECIONADO */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start md:self-auto bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md">
          <Layers className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" size={16} />
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">Licenças do Contrato:</span>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 bg-white px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-200">
            {selectedTenantId ? `${users.length} / ${maxUsersLimit} Utilizadores Utilizados` : 'Selecione uma Empresa'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FEEDBACK DE SUCESSO */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-center animate-fade-in shadow-none">
          <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0" />
          <span className="font-semibold">{successMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* FILTROS E BUSCA */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-center justify-between shadow-none">
        <div className="relative w-full md:max-w-md">
          <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3.5 pointer-events-none text-slate-400">
            <Search size={16} />
          </span>
          <input
            type="text"
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            placeholder={selectedTenantId ? "Pesquisar por nome, email ou nível..." : "Selecione uma empresa primeiro..."}
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-10 pr-4 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 w-full md:w-auto">
          <button
            onClick={() => fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)}
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            className="p-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            title="Recarregar Lista"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} className={loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>
          
          <button
            onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            title={!selectedTenantId ? "Selecione uma empresa primeiro" : "Criar utilizador para a empresa selecionada"}
            className="flex-1 md:flex-none bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white font-medium py-2 px-4 rounded-md transition-colors text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-none cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <Plus size={15} />
            <span>Novo Utilizador</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LISTA DE UTILIZADORES */}
      {!selectedTenantId ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center text-slate-500 shadow-none">
          <Building2 className="mx-auto text-slate-300 mb-3" size={40} />
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Selecione uma Empresa</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Por favor, escolha uma empresa no seletor acima para visualizar, editar e criar contas de utilizadores.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : loading ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center shadow-none">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
            <RefreshCw className="text-blue-700 animate-spin" size={28} />
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">A carregar utilizadores da empresa...</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : error ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-8 text-center text-red-700 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <AlertTriangle size={32} />
          <p className="text-xs">{error}</p>
          <button onClick={() => fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)} className="bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs px-4 py-2 rounded-md border border-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
            Tentar Novamente
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : filteredUsers.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center text-slate-500">
          <UsersIcon className="mx-auto text-slate-400 mb-3" size={36} />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhum utilizador encontrado para esta empresa.</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Clique em "+ Novo Utilizador" para adicionar o primeiro utilizador a este contrato.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Nome Completo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">E-mail</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Nível de Acesso (Cargo)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Escopo Operacional</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                {filteredUsers.map((u) => (
                  <tr key={u.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-xs border border-blue-200">
                        {u.fullName.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                      </div>
                      {u.fullName}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600">{u.email}</td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center w-fit px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium ${getRoleBadgeColor(u.role)}`}>
                          {formatRoleName(u.role)}
                        </span>
                        {u.role === 'technician' && u.specialty && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                            {u.specialty}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{formatScopeName(u.operational_scope)}</td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right space-x-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(u)}
                        className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        title="Editar Utilizador"
                      >
                        <Edit2 size={13} />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleConfirmDelete(u)}
                        className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 rounded-md border border-slate-300 hover:border-red-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        title="Eliminar Utilizador"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={13} />
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-lg shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white">
              <h2 className="text-md font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <UsersIcon className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                {modalMode === 'create' ? 'Novo Utilizador do Contrato' : 'Editar Informações do Utilizador'}
              </h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
              {/* Seleção de Empresa no Modal se não selecionado */}
              {modalMode === 'create' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Empresa (Tenant) *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={modalTenantId || selectedTenantId}
                    onChange={(e) => setModalTenantId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="">Selecione a empresa...</option>
                    {tenants.map((t) => (
                      <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                        {t.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Nome Completo *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <UserIcon size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: João Macamo"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  E-mail Corporativo *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <Mail size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: joao.macamo@empresa.co.mz"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Palavra-passe {modalMode === 'edit' && '(deixe em branco para manter)'}
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <Lock size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required={modalMode === 'create'}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={modalMode === 'create' ? 'Mínimo 6 caracteres' : '••••••••'}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Nível de Acesso (Cargo) *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={role}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const newRole = e.target.value as any;
                      setRole(newRole);
                      if (newRole === 'technician' && !specialty && technicianSpecialties.length > 0) {
                        setSpecialty(technicianSpecialties[0].value);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="tenant_admin">Gestor de Contrato (Admin)</option>
                    <option value="supervisor">Supervisor Operacional</option>
                    <option value="technician">Técnico de Manutenção</option>
                    <option value="driver_operator">Motorista / Operador</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Âmbito Operacional *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={operationalScope}
                    onChange={(e) => setOperationalScope(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="fleet">Frota</option>
                    <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CAMPO CONDICIONAL PARA TÉCNICO DE MANUTENÇÃO */}
              {role === 'technician' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Especialidade Técnica *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={specialty}
                    onChange={(e) => setSpecialty(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="">Selecione a especialidade...</option>
                    {technicianSpecialties.map((spec) => (
                      <option key={spec.value} value={spec.value}>
                        {spec.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors border border-slate-300 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {submitting ? (
                    <>
                      <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={13} />
                      <span>A gravar...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span>Guardar Alterações</span>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CONFIRMAÇÃO DE ELIMINAÇÃO */}
      {isDeleteModalOpen && deletingUser && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-sm shadow-xl p-6 text-center animate-slide-up">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <AlertTriangle size={24} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Eliminar Utilizador?</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-6">
              Tem a certeza de que deseja eliminar o utilizador <strong className="text-slate-900">{deletingUser.fullName}</strong>? 
              Esta ação removerá completamente o seu acesso e não pode ser desfeita.
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-3 justify-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleDeleteUser}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Sim, Eliminar
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default UsersPage;


// ===== src/pages/WorkOrders.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';

import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import {
  Plus,
  Wrench,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  Package,
  Sparkles,
  Info,
  DollarSign,
  Clock,
  Briefcase,
  User,
  Activity,
  Edit2,
  Eye,
  Settings,
  Play,
  Pause,
  ArrowRight,
  TrendingUp,
  GitCommit,
  Phone,
  UserCheck,
  Bell,
  Printer
} from 'lucide-react';
import { MaintenanceNotificationModal } from '../components/MaintenanceNotificationModal';

interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  category?: string;
  category_type?: string;
  type?: string;
  current_meter?: number;
  currentMeter?: number;
  meter_kind?: string;
  meterKind?: string;
}

interface Technician {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  specialty?: string;
  phone?: string;
  cost_per_hour?: number;
  costPerHour?: number;
}

interface TimelineEvent {
  id: string;
  eventType: 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'complete' | string;
  event_type?: string;
  reason?: string;
  justification?: string;
  performed_by?: string;
  performedBy?: string;
  created_at: string;
  createdAt?: string;
}

interface WorkOrder {
  id: string;
  wo_number: string;
  woNumber?: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  asset_id: string;
  assetId?: string;
  asset_name?: string;
  asset?: {
    code_tag?: string;
    name?: string;
  };
  order_type?: 'corrective' | 'preventive' | string;
  orderType?: string;
  priority: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent' | string;
  status: 'open' | 'in_progress' | 'waiting_parts' | 'paused' | 'blocked' | 'completed' | string;
  total_cost_mzn?: number;
  totalCostMzn?: number;
  parts_cost_mzn?: number;
  partsCostMzn?: number;
  labor_cost_mzn?: number;
  laborCostMzn?: number;
  downtime_hours?: number;
  downtimeHours?: number;
  resolution_notes?: string;
  resolutionNotes?: string;
  opened_at: string;
  openedAt?: string;
  assigned_to?: string;
  assignedTo?: string;
  technician_id?: string;
  technicianId?: string;
  meter_at_service?: number;
  meterAtService?: number;
}

export const WorkOrdersPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { counterLabel, failureCauses, technicianSpecialties, domain } = useCurrentDomain();
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<WorkOrder[]>([]);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [technicians, setTechnicians] = useState<Technician[]>([]);

  const domainAssets = assets.filter((asset: any) => {
    const isPlantType = asset.category === 'generator' || asset.category === 'machinery';
    const isFleetType = asset.category === 'vehicle_light' || asset.category === 'vehicle_heavy';
    if (domain === 'plant') return isPlantType;
    if (domain === 'fleet') return isFleetType;
    return true;
  });
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingTechs, setLoadingTechs] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Estados de pesquisa e filtragem
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [priorityFilter, setPriorityFilter] = useState('all');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [quickTab, setQuickTab] = useState<'all' | 'my_tasks' | 'pending' | 'in_progress' | 'completed' | 'maintenance_requests'>('all');

  // Solicitações de Manutenção / Notificações
  const [maintenanceRequests, setMaintenanceRequests] = useState<any[]>([]);
